defined_text = {
	name = GetGsmChg1m
	random = no
	text = {
		localization_key = GSM_CHG1M_Z
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = gsm_d_chg1m value = 0 } }
	}
	text = {
		localization_key = GSM_CHG1M_UR
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = gsm_d_chg1m value >= 0 } root = { NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_ui_green_up } } }
	}
	text = {
		localization_key = GSM_CHG1M_UG
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = gsm_d_chg1m value >= 0 } }
	}
	text = {
		localization_key = GSM_CHG1M_DG
		trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_ui_green_up } } }
	}
	default = GSM_CHG1M_DR
}
